# 都督piece 石河州 (display)
guanzhi_jie_z_000 = {
	type = character
	text = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = guanzhi_jie_zhou_000 var:guanzhi_jie_first = 0 }
		localization_key = gzxj_display_head_000
	}
	text = {
		trigger = { has_character_flag = guanzhi_jie_zhou_000 }
		localization_key = gzxj_display_more_000
	}
	text = {
		fallback = yes
		localization_key = guanzhi_ling_none
	}
}
